The 8 headers are SKU 코드 (SKU code), SKU명 (SKU name), 창고 (Warehouse), (전환 전 구분) 현재수량 ((Source) current qty), (전환 후 구분) 현재수량 ((Target) current qty), 전환수량 (Conversion qty), (전환 후 구분) 전환 후 수량 ((Target) qty after conversion), 메모 (Memo)<br>5. | TC_US134_74 | [Stock Quantity Calculation] Verify CS stock (CS-usable) is calculated correctly from CS stock (available) and pending CS re-delivery outbound quantity | 1. Log in as an INVENTORY_OPERATOR account with write permission<br>2. Test data exists:<br>- DEMO-SKU-0001 in CJ Incheon Aam: CS stock (available) 20; Pending CS re-delivery outbound quantity 6; Expected CS-usable 14<br>- DEMO-SKU-0008 in CJ Daegu warehouse: CS stock (available) 8; Pending CS re-delivery outbound quantity 5; Expected CS-usable 3<br>- DEMO-SKU-0011 in CJ Incheon Aam: CS stock (available) 5; Pending CS re-delivery outbound quantity 8; Expected CS-usable 0<br>3. Business rule: CS stock (CS-usable) = MAX(0, CS stock (available) - Pending CS re-delivery outbound quantity)<br>4. Run only once KakaoEnt's BRZ-14805 is on the test environment. Until then CS stock (CS-usable) equals CS stock (available) by design — record this TC as Blocked; the interim behaviour is TC_US134_75 | 1. Open 재고 관리 (Stock management)<br>2. Find DEMO-SKU-0001 in CJ Incheon Aam and read CS재고(가용재고) (CS stock — available) and CS재고(CS가능수량) (CS stock — CS-usable)<br>3. Calculate 20 - 6<br>4. Find DEMO-SKU-0008 in CJ Daegu warehouse and read CS stock (available) and CS stock (CS-usable)<br>5. Calculate 8 - 5<br>6. Find DEMO-SKU-0011 in CJ Incheon Aam and read CS stock (available) and CS stock (CS-usable)<br>7. Calculate 5 - 8 and apply the minimum value of 0<br>8. Compare each displayed CS stock (CS-usable) value with its calculated result | 1. The Stock Management screen opens successfully<br>2. DEMO-SKU-0001 displays CS stock (available) = 20 and CS stock (CS-usable) = 14<br>3. The calculated result is 20 - 6 = 14 and matches the displayed CS stock (CS-usable)<br>4. DEMO-SKU-0008 displays CS stock (available) = 8 and CS stock (CS-usable) = 3<br>5. The calculated result is 8 - 5 = 3 and matches the displayed CS stock (CS-usable)<br>6. DEMO-SKU-0011 displays CS stock (available) = 5 and CS stock (CS-usable) = 0<br>7. The raw result is 5 - 8 = -3; it is floored at 0, so no negative value is displayed<br>8. Every tested row satisfies CS stock (CS-usable) = MAX(0, CS stock (available) - Pending CS re-delivery outbound quantity) | Medium |  |  |  |  | Yes |  |  | Requirement: BR-9, 2.1 components 32 and 33, 1.A.7 BRZ-14805. Target rule — the interim limit is TC_US134_75. |  |

| TC_US134_234 | [Conversion] Verify source stock decreases and DEFECTIVE warehouse stock increases after scrapping on-hold stock | 1. Log in as an INVENTORY_OPERATOR account with write permission<br>2. The stock row DEMO-SKU-0001 in CJ Incheon Aam has 전체재고 (Total stock) 190 and 보류재고 (On-hold stock) 10<br>3. CJ Incheon Aam has its own DEFECTIVE virtual bucket (child warehouse) in the US069 warehouse master<br>4. DEMO-SKU-0001 has no stock row yet in that DEFECTIVE bucket (baseline 0)<br>5. Conversion quantity for this run: 5 | 1. Open 재고 관리 (Stock management) and find DEMO-SKU-0001 in CJ Incheon Aam<br>2. Read 전체재고 (Total stock) on that row and 보류재고 (On-hold stock) from the inventory data source because it is not a list column<br>3. Read the quantity of DEMO-SKU-0001 in CJ Incheon Aam's DEFECTIVE bucket<br>4. Tick the DEMO-SKU-0001 row and click 재고 전환 (Stock conversion)<br>5. Set 전환 구분 (Conversion type) to 불량 처리 (보류재고 → 불량) (Scrap as defective (On hold → Defective warehouse))<br>6. Enter 전환수량 (Conversion qty) 5 and click 저장 (Save)<br>7. When the list has reloaded, read 전체재고 (Total stock) on the DEMO-SKU-0001 row again and 보류재고 (On-hold stock) from the inventory data source because it is not a list column<br>8. Read the quantity of DEMO-SKU-0001 in CJ Incheon Aam's DEFECTIVE bucket again | 1. The DEMO-SKU-0001 row is shown for CJ Incheon Aam<br>2. 전체재고 (Total stock) reads 190 and 보류재고 (On-hold stock) reads 10<br>3. No stock row for DEMO-SKU-0001 is shown in that DEFECTIVE bucket, so the baseline is 0<br>4. The 재고 전환 (Stock conversion) dialog opens with 1 row<br>5. The dialog takes the choice and shows 불량 처리 (보류재고 → 불량) (Scrap as defective (On hold → Defective warehouse)) as the conversion type<br>6. The save goes through and 등록되었습니다 (Registered.) is shown<br>7. 보류재고 (On-hold stock) falls from 10 to 5 and 전체재고 (Total stock) falls from 190 to 185. A total still reading 190 means the build moved the quantity between buckets of the same row, so mark it FAIL — this direction takes the stock out of the warehouse altogether<br>8. A stock row for DEMO-SKU-0001 now exists in CJ Incheon Aam's DEFECTIVE bucket with 보류재고 (On-hold stock) = 5 and 가용재고 (Available stock) = 0. A build that puts it in 가용재고 means the goods can be picked for an order, so mark it FAIL | Critical | Passed |  |  |  | Yes | Automation |  | Requirement: AC 9, BR-1, BR-13 SCRAP_DEFECTIVE row, BR-13 destination table (physical source warehouse → its own DEFECTIVE bucket), BR-26b, EX-4, LIFECYCLE_CONTRACT #406. The only conversion direction that lowers the source warehouse's 전체재고 (Total stock). |  |

CS available 30, available 200, on-hold 20 — so 20 units moved from CS to on-hold only by going through available stock, in exactly 2 steps | Medium |  |  |  |  | Yes |  |  | Requirement: AC 9 constraint, BR-13, EX-4. |  |

Rows 1 and 3 are not committed; the inbound count is unchanged<br>4. Run B is rejected<br>5. ⚠ Open — SPEC-GAP-27 ②: the SRS does not yet state how the workbook checks 제조사 ID. WH-MANUFACTURER_BIZ_PARTNER_MISMATCH is a SKU-save rule (AC 27 ④) and is not the expected code here<br>6. The inbound count is still unchanged | High | Passed |  |  |  | Yes | Automation |  | Requirement: AC 27 ③, 1.A.4 WH-WAREHOUSE_BIZ_PARTNER_MISMATCH, 1.A.4 WH-MANUFACTURER_DIFFERENT, SPEC-GAP-27, AC 14 whole-workbook transaction, BR-26, SPEC-GAP-18. The headers 출고지 ID and 제조사 ID stay Korean — a header is a parse key and an English header makes the file fail to parse. | TC_US134_367 | [Adjustment Upload] Verify a negative adjustment quantity is rejected, and record what 0 does | 1. Log in as an INVENTORY_OPERATOR account that can write<br>2. A stock row for DEMO-SKU-0001 in CJ Incheon Aam whose 가용재고 (Available stock) is known<br>3. Three adjustment workbooks, identical apart from the 조정수량 cell: -5 with direction M, -5 with direction P, and 0 with direction P<br>4. The adjustment count and the stock value before each run are known | 1. Upload the workbook carrying -5 with direction M and record the outcome<br>2. Read 가용재고 (Available stock) for DEMO-SKU-0001 and compare it with the value before the run<br>3. Upload the workbook carrying -5 with direction P and record the outcome<br>4. Upload the workbook carrying 0 with direction P and record the outcome<br>5. Record whether any of the 3 produced a stock movement | 1. The workbook is rejected: a negative 조정수량 (Adjustment quantity) is refused, and the whole file is rejected, not only that row<br>2. 🔴 The stock value is unchanged. If it went up by 5 instead, the two minus signs cancelled: the row filed as a decrease raised the stock, the upload reported success, and 재고이력 (Stock history) holds a movement whose sign disagrees with the file — report it as Critical<br>3. Also rejected. The rule is on the sign of 조정수량, not on the direction paired with it — 증가(P)/차감(M) already carries the sign, so the quantity is a magnitude on both directions<br>4. ⚠ Record the outcome, do not pass or fail: 0 is reproduced as-is. Record whether it is accepted and whether it writes a zero-quantity 재고이력 row<br>5. Neither negative workbook produced a movement. A negative must be rejected on every door; 0 is reproduced AS-IS. Record the {cause} string the build returns |  |

| TC_US134_374 | [List Export] Verify the exported file carries the 20 data columns of the list in the list's order | 1. Log in as an INVENTORY_OPERATOR account that can write<br>2. The order is exactly: 상품코드 (Product code), SKU명 (SKU name), SKU코드 (SKU code), 바코드 (Barcode), 전체재고 (Total stock), 할당재고 (Allocated stock), 가용재고 (Available stock), CS재고(가용재고) (CS stock — available), CS재고(CS가능수량) (CS stock — CS-usable), 할당대기재고 (Stock awaiting allocation), 주문가능수량 (Orderable quantity), 입고예정수량 (Expected inbound quantity), 재고부족수량 (Stock shortage quantity), 미확보 출고수량 (Unsecured outbound quantity), SKU 유형 (SKU type), 창고 (Warehouse), 제조사 (Manufacturer), SKU ID, 비즈파트너 (Biz partner), 생성일 (Created date)<br>5. There is no extra column and no data column of the list is missing. The selection checkbox and 재고이력 (Stock history) are screen controls, not data, and are not in the file. SRS v1.10 still reads 22 (AC 15 → AC 1); the fix is in BA's local SRS v1.11, not yet published — re-check against v1.11 when it lands. |  |

| TC_US134_380 | [List Export] Verify whether the list export runs asynchronously, and record the figure if it does not | 1. Log in as an INVENTORY_OPERATOR account that can write<br>2. The stock list contains about 24400 rows with no filter applied<br>3. The browser Network tab is open | 1. Click 엑셀 다운로드 (Excel download) on the list with no filter applied, watching the network call<br>2. Note whether the call returns at once or holds the connection open until the file is ready<br>3. If it holds the connection, measure how long the call takes and write the figure down | 1. The call is sent and the header download panel opens<br>2. ⚠ Record which of the two it is. An immediate return is the expected asynchronous path. A held connection means the synchronous interim shipped and its named exception is live — report it rather than passing or failing this step<br>3. ⚠ Record the measured time. Dev measured 13 to 22 seconds for about 24,400 rows against a gateway timeout still to be confirmed, so the interim is safe only while the result set stays well below that. Report the figure rather than passing or failing this step | Medium |  |  |  |  | Yes |  |  | Requirement: EX-5, AC 15, BR-30, DEV-Q3. Record-only: a synchronous export is otherwise forbidden by AC 15 |  |

A second group is a FAIL: 상품코드 is unique across the whole table, not per biz partner, and the lookup throws if two rows come back<br>5. The group is unchanged - no SKU was appended to another partner's group<br>4. Nothing from the workbook was created. | TC_US134_428 | [Integration] Verify the server enforces the five guards the screen applies client-side | 1. Log in as an INVENTORY_OPERATOR account that can write<br>2. An authenticated API client is available<br>3. Send a physical SKU create call for biz partner Demo Partner A naming a manufacturer that belongs to Demo Partner B<br>5. PUT the saved set SKU group with a different component list and different quantities<br>6. Send a conversion call (policy SECURE_HOLD) and a CS-flag call, each naming the MIXED SKU | 1. The call is turned down with WH-SKU_NOT_SHIPPABLE: 재고로 관리되는 SKU가 아닙니다. - skuIds: {skuIds} (This SKU is not managed as stock. - skuIds: {skuIds}). A 201 here is a FAIL — accepting it explodes the MIXED SKU into components and produces one inbound request spanning 2 manufacturers<br>2. All 4 are turned down with WH-SKU_NOT_FOUND and the message 삭제된 SKU 입니다. - skuId: {skuId} (This SKU has been deleted. - skuId: {skuId}). ⚠ The conversion and CS-flag doors need the GetSkuIn port extended first; if the build does not carry it yet, record those 2 as Blocked, not as defects<br>3. The call is turned down with WH-WAREHOUSE_BIZ_PARTNER_MISMATCH [proposed]: SKU의 비즈파트너와 창고의 비즈파트너가 다릅니다. - warehouseId: {warehouseId}, bizPartnerId: {bizPartnerId} (The SKU's biz partner and the warehouse's biz partner differ. - warehouseId: {warehouseId}, bizPartnerId: {bizPartnerId}) — the destination must be a PLATFORM warehouse of the selection's own biz partner<br>4. The call is turned down with WH-MANUFACTURER_BIZ_PARTNER_MISMATCH [proposed]: 선택한 비즈파트너의 제조사가 아닙니다. - manufacturerId: {manufacturerId}, bizPartnerId: {bizPartnerId} (This manufacturer does not belong to the selected biz partner. - manufacturerId: {manufacturerId}, bizPartnerId: {bizPartnerId}) and no SKU is created<br>5. The call is turned down with WH-SKU_IMMUTABLE_FIELD [proposed] and the message SET 구성은 수정 시 추가하거나 수량을 변경할 수 없습니다. - skuGroupId: {skuGroupId} (Set components cannot be added or have their quantity changed in edit mode. - skuGroupId: {skuGroupId}) — a set's components cannot be changed in edit mode. Removing an already-deleted component stays allowed and must not be rejected<br>6. Both are turned down with WH-SKU_NOT_SHIPPABLE and the same message as step 1 | Critical |  |  |  |  | Yes |  |  | Requirement: AC 27 ① ② ③ ④ ⑤, BR-16, BR-17, BR-26, AC 5, AC 14, AC 19, component 58, components 136 and 137, 1.A.4 WH-SKU_NOT_SHIPPABLE, WH-SKU_NOT_FOUND deleted-SKU row, WH-WAREHOUSE_BIZ_PARTNER_MISMATCH, WH-MANUFACTURER_BIZ_PARTNER_MISMATCH, WH-SKU_IMMUTABLE_FIELD set-components row, OQ-19, LIFECYCLE_CONTRACT #407. ⚠ All 5 strings are as reported by Dev and not yet measured on the build; record the code and string returned and report a mismatch as a finding.
